######################################################
######   POD COMBAT MOVE REGISTRY
######################################################
# Writes the body of POD_initialize_combat_moves, which
# runs once at game start (called from
# common/on_action/POD_vanilla_game_start.txt) and registers
# every move story_cycle on Charon. Save-version mismatch is
# handled separately by POD_reload_combat_moves, which fires
# whenever the save's POD_combat_version differs from the
# script-side POD_current_combat_version. That constant is
# hand-bumped in pod_combat_values.txt, so regenerating this
# file does not by itself trigger a reload.
#
# Peer files:
#   pod_combat_move_init_effects.txt   - setters + constructor + reload
#   pod_combat_move_effects.txt        - execution engine

# ==========================================================
# TIER CONSTANTS
# ==========================================================
# Rebalance-in-one-place macros. Every move's numeric cost /
# damage / healing references one of these, so changing the
# constant's value rebalances every move using it.
# Outlier numeric values that don't fit any tier print as
# raw integers.

# AP costs (per-turn action points; base cap pod_combat_base_ap = 6, live ceiling pod_combat_effective_ap_cap_value)
@ap_cost_tiny	= 1
@ap_cost_minor	= 2
@ap_cost_medium	= 3
@ap_cost_major	= 4
@ap_cost_massive	= 5
@ap_cost_maximum	= 6

# Energy costs (splat-agnostic; 1 ~ 1 discipline tick).
@energy_cost_tiny	= 1
@energy_cost_minor	= 2
@energy_cost_medium	= 3
@energy_cost_major	= 4
@energy_cost_massive	= 5
@energy_cost_maximum	= 6
@energy_cost_seven	= 7
@energy_cost_eight	= 8
@energy_cost_nine	= 9
@energy_cost_ten	= 10

# Stress (CK3 resource). PC-only.
@stress_cost_minor	= 10
@stress_cost_medium	= 20
@stress_cost_major	= 40
@stress_cost_massive	= 80

# Gold (CK3 resource). PC-only.
@gold_cost_minor	= 15
@gold_cost_medium	= 50
@gold_cost_major	= 150
@gold_cost_massive	= 500

# Piety (CK3 resource). PC-only.
@piety_cost_minor	= 50
@piety_cost_medium	= 100
@piety_cost_major	= 250
@piety_cost_massive	= 500

# Prestige (CK3 resource). PC-only.
@prestige_cost_minor	= 50
@prestige_cost_medium	= 100
@prestige_cost_major	= 250
@prestige_cost_massive	= 500

# HP damage dealt to target.
@damage_tiny	= 1
@damage_minor	= 2
@damage_medium	= 3
@damage_major	= 4
@damage_massive	= 5
@damage_maximum	= 6

# Morale damage dealt to target (mental / intimidate).
@morale_damage_tiny	= 1
@morale_damage_minor	= 2
@morale_damage_medium	= 3
@morale_damage_major	= 4
@morale_damage_massive	= 5
@morale_damage_maximum	= 6

# Self-damage (reckless / drain moves).
@self_damage_tiny	= 1
@self_damage_minor	= 2
@self_damage_medium	= 3

# HP heal (self or ally).
@healing_tiny	= 1
@healing_minor	= 2
@healing_medium	= 3
@healing_major	= 4
@healing_massive	= 5

# Morale heal (rally / courage).
@morale_heal_tiny	= 1
@morale_heal_minor	= 2
@morale_heal_medium	= 3
@morale_heal_major	= 4
@morale_heal_massive	= 5

POD_initialize_combat_moves = {
	# Record the version  - POD_reload_combat_moves checks this
	# against the current POD_current_combat_version to detect
	# save-data mismatch and rebuild the global catalogue.
	set_global_variable = {
		name  = POD_combat_version
		value = POD_current_combat_version
	}

	# Validator suppressions (flag-sink, optional var SETs, log-entry
	# var USEs, event-target alias SETs, AoE burst-index marker, etc.)
	# all live in pod_combat_error_suppression_effects.txt and run once
	# at game start via POD_combat_suppress_validator_warnings. Don't add new
	# suppression blocks here  - extend the consolidated effect instead.

	# Per-move registry. Wrapped in hidden_effect_new_object so
	# the tooltip-preview pass that walks the configure-skills
	# decision's effect (via POD_reload_combat_moves) skips the
	# create_story + scope:new_combat_move setter chains  - 
	# preview can't resolve save_scope_as targets across the
	# create_story it skips. Real execution runs normally.
	hidden_effect_new_object = {
	####### Base Physical

	POD_init_combat_move = {
		DECISION   = POD_combat_move_strike
		SKILL_KEY  = strike
		CATEGORY   = ATK
		CONFIG     = base_physical
		GROUP      = universal
		AP_COST    = @ap_cost_major
		AI_WEIGHT  = 20
		TARGETING  = single_enemy
	}
	scope:new_combat_move = {
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = is_starter_move }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = uses_weapon }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_guard }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_spirit_gate }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_stance }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = requires_hit_roll }
		POD_combat_move_set_hp_damage = { VALUE = @damage_minor }


	}

	POD_init_combat_move = {
		DECISION   = POD_combat_move_heavy_blow
		SKILL_KEY  = heavy_blow
		CATEGORY   = ATK
		CONFIG     = base_physical
		GROUP      = universal
		AP_COST    = @ap_cost_maximum
		AI_WEIGHT  = 20
		TARGETING  = single_enemy
	}
	scope:new_combat_move = {
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = is_starter_move }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = uses_weapon }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_guard }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_spirit_gate }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_stance }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = requires_hit_roll }
		POD_combat_move_set_hp_damage = { VALUE = @damage_medium }
		POD_combat_move_set_soak_debuff_round = { TIER = 1 }
	}

	POD_init_combat_move = {
		DECISION   = POD_combat_move_quick_jab
		SKILL_KEY  = quick_jab
		CATEGORY   = ATK
		CONFIG     = base_physical
		GROUP      = universal
		AP_COST    = @ap_cost_medium
		AI_WEIGHT  = 20
		TARGETING  = single_enemy
	}
	scope:new_combat_move = {
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = is_starter_move }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = uses_weapon }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_guard }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_spirit_gate }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_stance }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = requires_hit_roll }
		POD_combat_move_set_hp_damage = { VALUE = @damage_tiny }
	}

	POD_init_combat_move = {
		DECISION   = POD_combat_move_reckless_charge
		SKILL_KEY  = reckless_charge
		CATEGORY   = ATK
		CONFIG     = base_physical
		GROUP      = universal
		AP_COST    = @ap_cost_massive
		AI_WEIGHT  = 20
		TARGETING  = single_enemy
	}
	scope:new_combat_move = {
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = is_starter_move }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = uses_weapon }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_guard }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_spirit_gate }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_stance }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = requires_hit_roll }
		POD_combat_move_set_hp_damage = { VALUE = @damage_major }
		POD_combat_move_set_self_damage = { VALUE = @self_damage_tiny }
	}

	####### Base Tactical

	POD_init_combat_move = {
		DECISION   = POD_combat_move_guard
		SKILL_KEY  = guard
		CATEGORY   = DEF
		CONFIG     = base_tactical
		GROUP      = universal
		AP_COST    = @ap_cost_medium
		AI_WEIGHT  = 8
		TARGETING  = self
	}
	scope:new_combat_move = {
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = is_starter_move }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_guard }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_spirit_gate }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = checks_stance }
		POD_combat_move_set_flag = { NAME = requires_hit_roll }

	}
